# Промпти артів карт

Готові промпти для генерації, по одному на карту. Файл збирає `tools/card-catalog/build-catalog.js`.

Сюжет кожної карти написаний після перегляду її спрайта в референсі: той самий типаж, поза й настрій, але свій дизайн. Стиль наш, а не референсу: похмура таро-ксилографія, чорна туш і кремова заливка на прозорому фоні. Шейдер сам тонує арт під матеріал рідкості, а силует незнайденої карти будується за контуром.

**Правила для кожного арту**

- Квадрат 1:1, прозорий фон (PNG з альфою), без рамки й тексту.
- Золотий перетин: головний акцент на лінії 38% або 62% ширини і 38% висоти, тіло йде золотою спіраллю вниз ліворуч.
- Нижній правий кут (25% ширини на 25% висоти) порожній, бо там стоїть медальйон стихії.
- Поле не менше 10% з кожного боку, нічого не обрізано.
- Сторона акценту чергується між картами, щоб колекція не виглядала однаково.
- Назву файлу й рядок у `client/public/card-art/card-art-map.txt` додавати за кодом картки: `код | назва | код`.

## Лицедійка Тінь (`actress_shadow`)

```text
Theatre actress holding a mask on a stick, shadow behind her a different shape, hidden dagger. Square 1:1 canvas. Golden ratio composition: the main focal point (eyes or the most important detail) sits on the left vertical golden line at about 38% of the width and the upper horizontal golden line at about 38% of the height. The body follows a golden spiral that sweeps down toward the lower left. Draw the subject smaller than you normally would, roughly centred, floating with generous empty transparent space on all four sides so that no part touches or is cut by any edge of the canvas. Pose the subject so its whole shape naturally tapers away from the lower right, leaving that corner as open background. Every form stays complete and organically drawn, with no straight cuts or stepped edges. Style: dark gothic tarot woodcut, like an old hand-carved medieval block print. Pure black ink only: heavy solid black shapes next to clean empty light areas, rough hand-cut contour lines, bold parallel hatching and crude cross-hatching, slightly grotesque character, large simple forms with few small details, no grey tones, no gradients, no airbrush. Light areas inside the subject are filled with flat warm cream paper colour, so the subject is opaque. Isolated subject on a fully transparent background, PNG with alpha, clean outer silhouette recognisable at 100 pixels, no card frame, no landscape unless named, no letters, no numbers, no text, no logo, no signature, no watermark. All human and humanoid characters are clearly adults. Every human or humanoid hand has exactly four digits in total: one thumb and three fingers, on both hands, including gloves and hands gripping objects. A visible closed grip has three curled finger bands and one opposing thumb, never four curled fingers plus a thumb. Hands follow the character's action through natural grips, fists or meaningful gestures; preserve the expressive pose while applying the four-digit anatomy, rather than displaying spread palms for counting. Use this four-digit stylization consistently while keeping shoulders, elbows, wrists and palms naturally connected, with no extra, missing, fused or duplicated limbs or digits. Women may be glamorous and alluring in bold fantasy outfits with bare shoulders, midriff and legs, without nudity.
```

Adult elven princess with exactly two arms in fine armor and a long gown sweeping down to the lower left, pointed ears, circlet, proud profile facing right, whole figure inside the central 80% of the canvas, nothing in the lower right. Bow construction must be physically exact: she holds a slender recurve bow upright at her side, not drawing it, with one continuous stave and one perfectly straight taut black string attached exactly at the top tip and exactly at the bottom tip, fully visible and unbroken, no arrow. Pure black ink and cream only, no other colours. Square 1:1 canvas. Golden ratio composition: the main focal point (eyes or the most important detail) sits on the right vertical golden line at about 62% of the width and the upper horizontal golden line at about 38% of the height. The body follows a golden spiral that sweeps down toward the lower left. Draw the subject smaller than you normally would, roughly centred, floating with generous empty transparent space on all four sides so that no part touches or is cut by any edge of the canvas. Pose the subject so its whole shape naturally tapers away from the lower right, leaving that corner as open background. Every form stays complete and organically drawn, with no straight cuts or stepped edges. Style: dark gothic tarot woodcut, like an old hand-carved medieval block print. Isolated subject on a fully transparent background, PNG with alpha, clean outer silhouette recognisable at 100 pixels, no card frame, no landscape unless named, no letters, no numbers, no text, no logo, no signature, no watermark. Every human or humanoid hand has exactly four digits in total: one thumb and three fingers, on both hands, including gloves and hands gripping objects. A visible closed grip has three curled finger bands and one opposing thumb, never four curled fingers plus a thumb. Hands follow the character's action through natural grips, fists or meaningful gestures; preserve the expressive pose while applying the four-digit anatomy, rather than displaying spread palms for counting. Use this four-digit stylization consistently while keeping shoulders, elbows, wrists and palms naturally connected, with no extra, missing, fused or duplicated limbs or digits.
